2026-11-05T08:49:06.3551581Z PUSH_OUTPUT="false"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3551797Z 
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3552102Z # workflow_call path (called from integration.yml). The planner only
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3552587Z # invokes us when a build is actually needed (no pin/cache match), so
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3553050Z # we always publish. The publish mode follows the caller's event:
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3553800Z # pull_request -> pr-scoped image (consumed by the calling Integration
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3554279Z # run via digest output, not by tag polling); main/schedule/dispatch
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3554805Z # -> pin-refresh image (operator opens a reviewed image-pin.json PR
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3555176Z # afterwards).
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3555439Z if [[ -n "$EXPECTED_FINGERPRINT" ]]; then
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3555737Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3556301Z   if [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "pull_request" && "$PR_HEAD_REPO" == "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" ]]; then
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3556844Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pr"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3557387Z     PR_IMAGE_TAG="${IMAGE_NAME}:pr-${PR_NUMBER}-${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3557835Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3558205Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3558575Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" || "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3558979Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3559207Z   else
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3559504Z     # Untrusted PR (fork) reaching workflow_call should have been
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3559917Z     # blocked at the planner; defend in depth here too.
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3560470Z     echo "::error::workflow_call invoked from unsupported event ($EVENT_NAME, head_repo=$PR_HEAD_REPO)." >&2
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3560964Z     exit 1
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3561162Z   fi
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3561470Z # Standalone path: push to main, weekly schedule, or manual dispatch.
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3561954Z # These refresh the reviewed image-pin.json digest. The pull_request
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3562422Z # trigger no longer exists on this workflow; PR builds reach us only
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3562816Z # through the workflow_call branch above.
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3563493Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3563879Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3564126Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"

2026-11-05T08:49:06.3564904Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3565188Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3565634Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="${DISPATCH_PUSH}"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3565946Z   if [[ "$DISPATCH_PUSH" == "true" ]]; then
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3566236Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3566462Z   fi
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3566641Z fi
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3566817Z {
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3567017Z   echo "source_sha=${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3567293Z   echo "short_sha=${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3567603Z   echo "cache_key=ijt-browser-ci-${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-05T08:49:06.3567912Z   echo "push=${PUSH_OUTPUT}"
